\subsection{53. Where the energy-row floor of section 50 comes from (2026-09-30; the cause is in section 58)}\label{53-where-the-energy-row-floor-of-section-50-comes-from-2026-09-30-the-cause-is-in-section-58}

The floor of section 50 (hydrodynamic solves ending at the iteration cap
with \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} 1e-5 to 1e-4 against the energy tolerance 1e-6) was measured on
the 0.06 conduction rung at factor 0.773561 (IC: pass state
r20260930T172935.418\_c1790756975418473\_p22 of
\texttt{atomic\_scalar\_gj1132x0.06\_kzz1e9\_HeH9.7\_ser2\_cond08\_s0.773561}; runs in
\texttt{.P1/atomic97/diag\_R/floor\_*}, binaries \texttt{EXHALE\_9d0b8d4c.x} and, for the
new diagnostics below, \texttt{EXHALE\_dfb51387.x}). Every run starts at the same
\textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} = 3.882e-5, energy row of cell 296.

\textbf{The row.} At cells 290 to 312 (r = 1.75 to 2.06 R\_p) the energy row is
the difference of the advective divergence and the conduction source, each
\textasciitilde1e-10 in the row\textquotesingle s units, with heating \textasciitilde4e-12; the residual is +-1e-15 to
4e-15, alternating in sign from cell to cell, i.e. 1e-5 to 4e-5 of the row\textquotesingle s
scale. The temperature there is 748 K and flat: the relative first
difference of T is 4.3e-5 to 5.3e-5 per cell and the relative second
difference 1.4e-6 to 2.1e-6 per cell. The H ionization crosses x(H+) = 0.5
between cells 306 and 307.

\textbf{Excluded, by measurement.}

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
{\small
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
candidate & test & result \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
acceptance rule & \texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_SER\_RNORM} (section 50) & same floor \\
scaling of the linear model & \texttt{EXHALE\_JUDGED\_ROWS}, \texttt{EXHALE\_JUDGED\_MERIT} & cap at 1.5e-5 / 5.3e-5 \\
probe length of the Jacobian action & \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_PROBE\_ARC} = 10, 100; \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_COLUMN\_SCALE=1} & 40 iterations, best 8.2e-6 / 1.0e-5 / 5.4e-5, no descent \\
frozen WENO3 weights in the model & new \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_FRESH\_WEIGHTS=1} (products with the weights recomputed) & first 9 iterations identical to 4 digits: the Krylov solve takes 1 product, the step is the banded preconditioner\textquotesingle s \\
tolerance of the per-cell ionization solve & \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_TOL} = 1e-12, 1e-14 & start \\
sonic point, Roe entropy fix & Mach number of the band & 0.005 to 0.013: subsonic, the Roe flux is smooth there \\
\end{longtable}
}
}

The additivity test (\texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_ADDITIVITY=1}) puts its defect in cells 1
to 32 (mass and momentum rows; along directions off the Krylov basis the
second difference does not scale with the arc at cell 1), not in the band.
That non-smoothness at the base is recorded here and not pursued.

\textbf{Located.} \texttt{EXHALE\_LINEAR\_ROWS=1} now prints, cell by cell over a band
(\texttt{EXHALE\_LINEAR\_ROWS\_CELLS=lo,hi}, default 290,312), the energy row F, the
model\textquotesingle s F + J dY, the row actually found at Y + dY, their ratio, and the
difference quotient (F(Y + t dY) - F)/t for t = 0.1 to 1e5. (The
"linear residual left over the row of F" printed before is the column
maximum in raw units, which the base cells set; it says nothing about this
band.) On the first iteration:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  cells 290 to 304 and 308 to 312: actual change / predicted = 0.90 to
  1.13, the row falls by \textasciitilde10 per step;
\item
  cells 305, 306, 307: 1.91, 2.05, 1.41; the row changes sign at the same
  magnitude (306: +1.42e-15 -\textgreater{} -1.49e-15) and does so again on the next
  iteration (ratios 2.03, 4.62, 0.43).
\end{itemize}

The difference quotient along dY is constant within 10 to 20 per cent for
t \textgreater= 10 and at t = 0.1, and doubles at t = 1 in cells 305 to 307 only
(306: -1.28, \textbf{-2.91}, -1.59, -1.39, -1.43, -1.42, -1.44 e-15): the row
carries a deterministic jump of 0.6 to 1.5e-15 between states 1e-12 apart,
i.e. 1e-5 of its scale. Heating and cooling in cell 306 jump by 3e-11
(relative) at the same t, and the H I / H II fractions of cells 306 and 307
by 1e-11 to 2e-11, ten times their neighbors\textquotesingle, with a relative step of
\textasciitilde1e-12 in every cell.

\textbf{The source.} With \texttt{EXHALE\_EQ\_MEASURE\_TRACE=1} every residual evaluation
stops the composition elimination after 1 pass, on a particle-count
increment of 6e-12 at cell 307 (the closure target is 1e-8). Forcing more
passes (\texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_EQ\_TOL=1e-15}, \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_MAX=12}), the
increment at cell 307 stays at 3.0e-11 for all 12 passes: the elimination
does not converge there. \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_REPORT\_CELL=307} shows x(H+) of that
cell alternating between two values on successive passes,

\begin{verbatim}
5.030758860692102E-01   5.030758860599341E-01   (difference 9.3e-12)
\end{verbatim}

each returned with a reaction residual of 1e-16 and solver info 1; with the
per-cell tolerance at 1e-15 the two values are the same to the last digit
and the solver returns info 4 (no further progress). \emph{Likely} the elimination alternates between two nearby states. It is
not established that the per-cell residual is flat (section 54): the
printed residual is the normalized one, whose hydrogen turnover bound
includes the Penning term with the total He density, and successive
passes change the coefficients of the cell problem (T, n\_e, attenuation),
so the two x values can be roots of two slightly different problems.

\textbf{The amplification.} An indeterminacy of 1e-11 in the composition is an
indeterminacy of 1e-11 in the particle count and in T = p/((n\_tot + n\_e) k).
Where the row is the balance of advection and conduction on a profile whose
relative curvature is 1.7e-6 per cell, that moves the conduction divergence
by 1e-11/1.7e-6 = 6e-6 of itself, which is the row\textquotesingle s scale. MEASURED jumps
0.6 to 1.5e-15 against a scale of 1e-10 agree with this. Newton cannot
bring the row below the jump; the solve therefore wanders at 1e-5 to 1e-4
and a rung certifies only when one pass lands inside the tolerance
(section 50).

\textbf{The same place in the other chains.} The worst energy cell of the last
40 iterations against the x(H+) = 0.5 crossing:

{\def\LTcaptype{none} % do not increment counter
{\small
\begin{longtable}[]{@{}lll@{}}
\toprule\noalign{}
rung & worst energy cells (count of 40) & crossing between cells \\
\midrule\noalign{}
\endhead
\bottomrule\noalign{}
\endlastfoot
0.0624, factor 0.913 & 297 (30), 290, 296, 292 & 296 / 297 \\
photochemical descent, x = 0.0634 & 287 (20), 284, 282, 299 & 288 / 289 \\
0.06, factor 0.774 & 301 (10), 303, 308, 307 & 306 / 307 \\
\end{longtable}
}
}

\textbf{Not decided, for the user.} (i) Condition the per-cell ionization
residual at x(H+) \textasciitilde{} 0.5, so that the composition is determined to rounding
there. (ii) The energy row\textquotesingle s certification scale in a conduction-dominated
layer: it is the largest of the row\textquotesingle s terms, and the conduction term is
itself the difference of two face fluxes that are, by the first and second
differences above, \textasciitilde30 times larger; measured against the face fluxes the
same jump would be \textasciitilde2e-7 of scale (an estimate from those differences, not
measured). Neither was changed. Remedy (ii) is withdrawn (section 54): a
larger denominator admits a 30 times larger absolute imbalance and does not
make the physical residual smaller.

\textbf{Also seen.} \texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_TRACE=8} from a second seed (the column\textquotesingle s
composition shifted by five cells) converges at fixed Y to a different
composition (max relative difference 4.8 at cell 266, \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} 1.78 against
3.9e-5). The shifted seed changes the elemental totals too (hydrogen at
cell 144 by 37 per cent, section 54), so the two runs solve different
elemental problems. They do not show multiple fixed points of one
problem.

\textbf{Code.} \texttt{EXHALE\_JV\_FRESH\_WEIGHTS=1} (steady\_newton.f90, measurement only)
and the band and ladder printout of \texttt{EXHALE\_LINEAR\_ROWS} (one residual
evaluation per ladder length) were added; with neither key set the run is
unchanged (iteration 1 of the diagnostic run reproduces \textbar\textbar R\textbar\textbar{} = 1.945e-5).
The comment of \texttt{residual\_norms} (steady\_residual.f90) said the two regions\textquotesingle{}
rows are made dimensionless by different physics; \texttt{residual\_row\_scale} has
no region switch, and the comment now says so. The chains were not touched.

of sections 51 and 53 and of the handoff that the review showed to be

}
}

\subsection{55. One failure contract for the advection-derived product (2026-10-01)}\label{55-one-failure-contract-for-the-advection-derived-product-2026-10-01}

Plan \texttt{md/PLAN\_20260930\_rev3.md} A1, A2 and A6; the defect is finding 4.3 of
section 54 (a rejected column energy solve left its rows labeled corrected
and the evaluation exited 0). Implemented by the worker and checked on the
diff and the runs.

\textbf{The column solve} (\texttt{energy\_column\_newton}, \texttt{post\_process\_adv.f90})
returns an outcome: \emph{rejected} with its reason (linear solve, nonfinite,
non-positive T, metal temperature band, iteration cap) and the triggering
cell, or \emph{step\_passed\_unverified} (the step test passed; no residual is
formed after the last step, so it is not called converged). A rejected
column keeps the marching profile and writes its WHOLE unknown set
\texttt{adv\_failed} (decision D2(i): no schema change; the ghost rows are written
failed but not counted). For a metal-band rejection the log prints the
triggering cell\textquotesingle s energy row term by term in erg cm\^{}-3 s\^{}-1, at the column\textquotesingle s
T and at the run\textquotesingle s T (R = q0 fv/(mup mum dr\_step), the scaling of
\texttt{T\_equation}).

\textbf{The derived-state record.} \texttt{post\_process\_adv} returns \texttt{adv\_derived\_state}
(chemistry, column outcome and reason, physical-cell counts, \texttt{outer\ =\ unverified}: the outer loop is still ten fixed passes, \texttt{n\_pp\_passes}); both
callers print it and \texttt{write\_output} writes it as \texttt{\#\ adv\_derived\_state:\ ...}
into both \texttt{*\_adv} headers (schema and columns unchanged; the regression
comparison excludes \texttt{\#} lines). The evaluate route exits 7 when the record
is rejected (2 stays the certification refusal of the input state, written
first; 7 is used by nothing else in \texttt{src/} or the shell consumers).
\texttt{read\_adv\_validity} (\texttt{exhale\_transit\_lib.py}) refuses a row when
\texttt{adv\_T\_status\ /=\ 0} OR \texttt{adv\_comp\_status\ =\ 2}, returns \texttt{refused\_reason} and
\texttt{derived\_state} (None = unknown); \texttt{EXHALE\_transit.py} prints the record and
refuses a rejected \texttt{adv} spectrum unless \texttt{EXHALE\_TRANSIT\_DIAGNOSTIC=1}, which
writes the refusal into the spectrum metadata. \texttt{ew\_of\_state.sh} requires the
binary, a fresh work directory, checks every step, records the md5 and
\texttt{state\_id} of both state files, the binary md5 and the \texttt{EXHALE\_*} overrides,
judges \texttt{solution} and \texttt{adv} separately, and states the EW in
percent-angstrom (the former \texttt{mA?} was wrong). \texttt{run\_case.sh} accepts 7 as
"solved state certified, adv product refused": the state is published with
its certification, the refusal and the verdict line go into \texttt{run.json}
(\texttt{adv\_product}, \texttt{adv\_derived\_state}), REPRODUCE.md and the last log line,
the transit pass is skipped (the transit tool itself exits 7 on a rejected
record) and earlier \texttt{tpm\_*} products of the case are moved to
\texttt{superseded\_case\_products/} so that none can be read as this run\textquotesingle s line
measure; an unknown exit still fails the pass. Launcher tests
(\texttt{LHS1140b/models/tests/}): \texttt{evaluate\_entry.sh} 25/25 (the five exit-7
checks fail on the former tools), \texttt{runner\_records.sh} 18/18 after a fixture
defect of its own was fixed (its synthetic tree lacked
\texttt{he10830\_equivalent\_width.py} and the observation CSV that \texttt{status.py}
imports; 14/18 on the former fixture), and the other five launcher suites
unchanged.

\textbf{A6.} After every composition update (the H/He sweep, the metal re-solve
and its failure branch) \texttt{n\_tot}, \texttt{n\_e}, \texttt{mmw} and the caloric quantities are
refreshed, then \texttt{xion}, then heating, the energy solve and \texttt{p\_out}.
\texttt{wasp\_full} with \texttt{pp\_metals\ 2}: max \textbar p - (n\_tot + n\_e) k T\textbar/p of the written
composition 4.4e-8 -\textgreater{} 7.8e-16; the \texttt{\_adv} T column moves by 1.0e-6. The
\texttt{xion} refresh acts in every mode: on the fiducial atomic full-equation
state the outer passes now reach their fixed point (column iterations
20..4 -\textgreater{} 20..1 over the ten passes) and the \texttt{\_adv} T moves by 2.9e-10.
\textbf{Separate defect found and fixed:} the \texttt{pp\_metals\ =\ 2} metal re-solve
never set \texttt{ieq\_cell\%T\_K}, so its charge exchange ran at the temperature of
the last equilibrium cell and the \texttt{cx\_require\_cell} guard aborted every
\texttt{pp\_metals\ =\ 2} run (reproduced on \texttt{wasp\_full}; one line).

\textbf{The M2 rejection explained.} \texttt{ew\_M2\_s1} (molecular photochemical
fiducial, section 51): reason \texttt{metal\_T\_band} at cell 437 (r = 10.35 R\_p) on
10 of 10 passes: the column\textquotesingle s T 2445 K is 2.007 times the run\textquotesingle s 1218 K. The
row at 2445 K closes to 9.5e-12 of its largest term (compression work
5.6e-13 = photoheating 3.7e-13 + conduction 1.6e-13; radiative cooling
1e-16), and with that cell alone put back at 1218 K the conduction from its
neighbors (7.9e-10) leaves a residual equal to the largest term. So 2445 K
is a root of the corrected, conduction-coupled equation and only the band
refuses it; the band is a guard of the cell-by-cell Brent solve against
spurious hot metal-cooling roots and has no stated validity for the column
solve. Whether the column is to be accepted on its residual instead (plan
B2) is held for the user; the band is unchanged and the case stays
rejected (EW 1.1032 diagnostic; the \texttt{solution} spectrum 8.563 is a result).

\textbf{Checks.} \texttt{src/tests/adv\_static\_limit}: 53 -\textgreater{} 63 PASS, 0 FAIL, with the
gfortran and the ifx binary (rows K: a stored certified state evaluated
plain and with each of the five injected rejections,
\texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_TEST\_REJECT=\textless{}reason\textgreater{}}, measurement-only; rows L: the reader on
composition-only failure, retained/unsupported/not-evaluated kept apart, an
absent record read as unknown). Evaluate-only reruns in
\texttt{.P1/fiducial\_conduction/verify\_w1/}: M2 exit 7 with the record above;
fiducial atomic exit 0, \texttt{step\_passed\_unverified}, EW 0.6491 unchanged.
Known and left as it is: a gfortran runtime error exits 2, the code of the
certification refusal (TO\_BE\_DONE (o)).

\subsection{56. Continuation factors written exactly; legacy F7.5 tokens read as intervals (2026-10-01)}\label{56-continuation-factors-written-exactly-legacy-f75-tokens-read-as-intervals-2026-10-01}

Finding 5 of section 54 (plan A3). \texttt{load\_IC.f90} writes a factor s /= 1 of
\texttt{cond} and \texttt{interdiff\_enth} as \texttt{ES23.16E3} (\texttt{factor\_token\_text}, \texttt{adjustl};
s = 1 stays \texttt{T}), and the writer, reader, comparison and absent-token values
share one length, 32 (previously 8/16/16/8); with both factors the options
field is 296 of its 1024 characters, and a field or token that would not fit
is refused, never cut. A legacy F7.5 token (\texttt{factor\_token\_kind}, these two
tokens only) states the interval {[}v - 5e-6, v + 5e-6{]}: a requested factor
inside it loads the state as a seed with the claim dropped
(\texttt{legacy\_factor\_token}, one printed line, a \texttt{\#\ legacy\_factor\_token} history
line keeping the original text); a factor outside it is a physics change
under the existing rule (named on \texttt{Restart\ option\ change:}, or refused).
Malformed values are refused even when named. The run log
(\texttt{conduction\_scale}, \texttt{interdiffusion\_enthalpy\_scale}) and the setup report
print the factor \texttt{ES13.6} and the report names a migration. gfortran and
ifx write 0.773561, 1e-6, 1 - 1e-12 and 0 as the same token text and each
reloads the other\textquotesingle s state at the same factor. Test \texttt{restart\_factor}
(grid\_and\_gates 25): 38/38 PASS with both compilers (15 of the first 27
FAIL on 60a90fb); the gate tests of section 39 unchanged (gfortran 119
PASS, 11 XFAIL, 0 FAIL; the 16 ifx FAILs are the fixtures\textquotesingle{} 1-ulp compiler
difference, present before). \texttt{grid\_and\_gates/run.sh} uses \texttt{-module} and
\texttt{-qmkl} for ifx (before, ifx could not link the Fortran drivers). Round
trip: rho, v, p bitwise (gfortran) or 1 ulp (ifx), T and species 1-3 ulp,
the loader\textquotesingle s own arithmetic. States written on 2026-09-29/30 with F7.5
tokens (every chain rung) keep them and load only as seeds.

\subsection{57. The H2 conductivity without overflow (2026-10-01)}\label{57-the-h2-conductivity-without-overflow-2026-10-01}

Finding 10.2 of section 54 (plan A4). \texttt{h2\_conductivity} evaluated
E(x) = x\^{}2 e\^{}x/(e\^{}x - 1)\^{}2 (x = 5987 K/T) as written: (e\^{}x - 1)\^{}2 overflows
above x \textasciitilde{} 355 (T \textless{} 16.9 K), where the form returned 0 in place of
E \textless{} 1e-148, and x\^{}2 e\^{}x above x = 696.69, so 8.553 K \textless= T \textless{} 8.593 K gave
NaN, and \texttt{thermal\_conductivity} added n\_H2 x NaN even where n\_H2 = 0. (The
review and section 54 had said "NaN above x \textasciitilde{} 355"; the NaN interval is the
narrow one.) E is now \texttt{einstein\_heat\_capacity}: x\^{}2 e\^{}-x/(1 - e\^{}-x)\^{}2 for
x \textgreater= 0.05, the series 1 - x\^{}2/12 + x\^{}4/240 - x\^{}6/6048 below (truncation
\textless{} 2.3e-16; the two agree at the switch to 6.7e-16), 0 above x = 700;
\texttt{h2\_conductivity} stops on a non-positive or non-finite T; the H2 term is
formed only where n\_H2 \textgreater{} 0, in the same summation order, so an atomic
mixture is bitwise unchanged. Fit and range (200-2000 K) unchanged.
\texttt{src/tests/energy\_update}: 54 -\textgreater{} 69 PASS (8.57 K = 1.3235866213750098e3,
200/748/2000 K unchanged to 1e-14, the 1e6 K series limit, continuity,
n\_H2 = 0 mixture bitwise atomic). Stationary evaluate of the molecular
scalar He/H 1.45 conduction state, HEAD against HEAD + fix (ifx):
\texttt{Hydro\_ioniz} and \texttt{Ion\_species} bitwise; \texttt{*\_adv} at rounding (T 7e-14).

\subsection{58. The energy-row floor was a rounding staircase of the absorbed fraction (2026-10-01)}\label{58-the-energy-row-floor-was-a-rounding-staircase-of-the-absorbed-fraction-2026-10-01}

Plan C1-C3, on the state of section 53 (\texttt{diag\_R/floor\_jvadd}). Keys added,
default off: \texttt{EXHALE\_IEQ\_DUMP\_CELL=\textless{}j\textgreater{}} writes every He 2\^{}3S-branch solve of
cell j to \texttt{ieq\_cell\_dump.txt} (tags, T, densities, every rate coefficient,
start and returned vectors, dimensional rows, turnover bounds, normalized
residual, the forward-difference Jacobian) and the cell\textquotesingle s photoionization
rates at perturbed entry states to \texttt{ieq\_cell\_rate\_probe.txt};
\texttt{EXHALE\_RESID\_SC\_TRACE\_SEED=shift\textbar{}h2\textbar{}partition} selects seed B of the
two-seed trace (\texttt{partition}: the elemental inventory and the charge closure
kept, a fraction of H I and He I moved into H II and He II). A5:
\texttt{report\_accepted\_cell\_state} names unknown 4 by the system (He 2\^{}3S in the
atomic triplet branch). With every key unset the outputs equal the HEAD
build\textquotesingle s.

\textbf{Verdict: hypothesis (b) of section 53, and the cause.} At cell 307 the
elimination alternated between x(H+) = 0.50307588606921 and
0.50307588605993 (1.8e-11 apart). The dumped rows are reproduced from the
dumped coefficients in binary64 (146 records, to 1.8e-17 of the turnover
bound). Each returned x is the 50-digit root of ITS OWN pass\textquotesingle s coefficients
to 1 ulp and 1.4e-11 (normalized) from the other\textquotesingle s; the binary64 residual is
monotone across 41 ulps, not flat. The coefficients differ because P\_HI,
the cell\textquotesingle s photoionization rate, is a staircase function of the cell\textquotesingle s own
entry n(H I): steps of 4-5e-11 relative every 1.7e-11 in n(H I).
Cause: \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_per\_unit\_depth} (\texttt{utilities.f90}) took the closed
form (1 - e\^{}-d)/d for d \textgreater= 1e-8, whose rounding is a staircase with
relative steps eps/d (1.5e-9 at this cell\textquotesingle s d = 1.5e-7). The smooth
composition map contracts (-0.073 per pass, measured with the partition
seed), but its fixed point lay on a step, so a period-2 orbit resulted, and
that orbit was the floor: two seeds end on it in opposite phase,
\textbar\textbar R\_B - R\_A\textbar\textbar{} = 1.7e-5. The "flat residual" of section 53 is withdrawn.

\textbf{Fix.} The Taylor series through d\^{}8 (Horner) for 1e-8 \textless= d \textless{} 1e-2
(truncation \textless{} 3e-25), the closed form above (error \textless= 2.2e-14); the old
comment\textquotesingle s truncation figure for the three-term series at 1e-8 was 4e-25 and
is 4.2e-26. Kahan\textquotesingle s (1 - u)/(-ln u) does not work: ifx -O3 -xHost folds
ln(exp(-d)) to -d and the output was bitwise the closed form\textquotesingle s. Reach: every
XUV photoionization and photoheating rate (\texttt{photoionization\_field\_at\_cell\_H}
and \texttt{\_HHe} through \texttt{cell\_mean\_attenuation}, the a\_tau = 0 branch) and the
H2O/OH FUV band rates. Profiles move by \textless= 8e-11 (x(H+)), 1.3e-11 (T),
2.7e-10 (mean mass flux): far below the 0.1 per cent rule.

\textbf{Result.} floor\_jvadd with the chain settings (\texttt{EXHALE\_PTC\_SER=2}, cap
400, omega 1, factor 0.773561): ONE outer pass, 2 JFNK steps, info = 0,
CERTIFIED, energy row 1.16e-7 (tolerance 1e-6), 1.3 s; the HEAD build with
the same settings: 400 iterations, info = 1, energy row 3.56e-5, still
9.0e-6 after three passes, never certified. Test \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_switch}
(physics\_probe 28): 14 assertions (quad-precision error on each side,
continuity at both switches, mpmath at 1.5e-7, 1e-3, 0.1, 1, 10, no
staircase over 41 points at 1.5e-7), all pass with gfortran and ifx at -O0;
the former form fails 5. Runs in \texttt{diag\_R/w4\_*}; figures in
\texttt{diag\_R/w4\_figures/}.

Consequence for the chains (stopped 2026-10-01, section 55 of the handoff):
their slow rungs were this staircase, not the acceptance rule or the
stopping measure of the elimination (plan C4, which stays a comment defect
of that measure, not a cure).

\subsection{59. One (1 - e\^{}-x)/x for the code, and the other small-value forms (2026-10-01)}\label{59-one-1---e-xx-for-the-code-and-the-other-small-value-forms-2026-10-01}

\texttt{one\_minus\_exp\_over\_x} (\texttt{utilities.f90}: the series through x\^{}8 below 1e-2,
the closed form above, error \textless= 2.2e-14) is now the only evaluation of the
quotient. Evaluated through it, each with its own name, limits and meaning:
\texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_per\_unit\_depth} (section 58), \texttt{absorbed\_fraction\_over\_tau}
and \texttt{absorbed\_photon\_fraction} (\texttt{util\_ion\_eq.f90}: the case-A/B ground
capture weights and the on-the-spot absorption of recombination radiation;
former switch 1e-4 with errors 2.2e-12 and 4e-14), the thin branch of
\texttt{line\_escape\_probability\_one\_face} (\texttt{Cool\_coeff.f90}, the metal and helium
fine-structure line escape; former closed form from 1e-8, error up to
2e-8), and the Ly-alpha Sobolev escape \texttt{beta\_sob} (\texttt{lya\_rt.f90}), which
returned 0 in place of 1 for tau\_S below \textasciitilde1e-16 and at tau\_S = 0 (a limit
defect; reached only with excited hydrogen on, which the LHS 1140 b runs do
not have). Then the three forms that lose accuracy where their value is
small: the stimulated-emission factor 1 - e\^{}-Ek/T of
\texttt{line\_center\_opacity\_lte} and the absorbed Lyman-continuum fraction of
\texttt{excited\_hydrogen.f90} as y (1 - e\^{}-y)/y (limit y), and the lower
incomplete gamma functions of the l-mixing rate, \texttt{lower\_gamma\_2} and
\texttt{lower\_gamma\_3}, as their power series in Horner form below 0.25 (through
x\^{}12) and 0.5 (through x\^{}17), closed form above, errors \textless= 1.1e-14 (the
former three-term series switched at 1e-2 with jumps of 6.7e-8 and 8.4e-8
there). \texttt{fine\_structure\_escape\_probability} takes its thin reference in
quadruple precision (its double reference was the cancelling form).
Test \texttt{one\_minus\_exp\_over\_x\_sites} (physics\_probe 46): 87 assertions
(mpmath values, continuity at every switch, no staircase over 41-point
scans, the limits), all pass with gfortran and ifx at -O0; the former code
fails 36. Evaluate-only: floor\_jvadd moves \textless= 5.7e-14 beyond section 58;
the molecular photochemical s1 state \textless= 1.2e-10 (H II, O II at a ghost
cell), CERTIFIED before and after (mass 2.7e-9, energy 2.3e-8); the last
three forms change neither run (excited hydrogen off; y of order 0.1-1 on
the closed-form side).

\subsection{60. The combined build and the tests of sections 55-59 (2026-10-01)}\label{60-the-combined-build-and-the-tests-of-sections-55-59-2026-10-01}

The tree with every edit of sections 55-59 built with gfortran (md5
a68670605f8d55c3a84e8c5ce4e04ca8) and ifx (fe48f7b774aac4490bd00334644fca6b);
the suites the edits touch, run once on that tree: \texttt{energy\_update} 69 PASS,
0 FAIL; \texttt{grid\_and\_gates} 423 PASS, 12 XFAIL, 0 FAIL (every program passed);
\texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 63 PASS, 0 FAIL with the gfortran binary and 63/63 with
the ifx binary; \texttt{physics\_probe} 2295 PASS, 0 FAIL. After section 61 the tree
is rebuilt (\texttt{EXHALE.x} aacba6fd93e4a547902ae732ad0b9bb2, \texttt{EXHALE\_ifx.x}
bff027c3c4ab416a429b30b5b70e9a00) and \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} (62 PASS, rows K
with five reasons), \texttt{energy\_update} and \texttt{evaluate\_entry.sh} rerun on it
(counts in section 61). The launcher suites of \texttt{LHS1140b/models/tests/} were run
by the worker of section 55 (\texttt{evaluate\_entry.sh} 25/25, \texttt{runner\_records.sh}
18/18, five others unchanged). The regression matrix was not run and no
golden was touched (user instruction of 2026-09-29; the measured movements
of every change are below 1e-6 relative, sections 55-59). The workers\textquotesingle{}
intermediate binaries (\texttt{EXHALE\_w*.x}, \texttt{build\_w*/}, \texttt{diag\_R/w4\_bin/}) are
superseded by these two.

\subsection{61. The column energy solve accepted on its residual; the refused rows carry the solution (2026-10-01)}\label{61-the-column-energy-solve-accepted-on-its-residual-the-refused-rows-carry-the-solution-2026-10-01}

User decision of 2026-10-01 ("잔차"): the conduction-coupled column of the
advected profile is judged on its residual, not on the metal temperature
band (TO\_BE\_DONE (p), plan rev3 B2). Implemented by the worker of section
55 and checked on the diff, the tests and the three evaluate-only runs.

\textbf{Acceptance.} After the iteration stops (step 1e-10 or 100 iterations, a
stopping rule only) the transport terms are formed again at the final
profile, and every unknown\textquotesingle s energy row R = q0 fv/(mup mum dr\_step) must
satisfy \textbar R\textbar{} \textless= atol\_E + cert\_tol\_energy S\_E, with S\_E the largest of the
grouped terms (div(rho e v) as one term, p div v, heating, cooling, the
conduction divergence, the element and the carrier enthalpy divergences)
and atol\_E = 10 eps times the parts the row is assembled from (the grouped
terms, the two advective halves, the three conduction face terms). MEASURED:
the stagnated floor is 0.73 to 0.99 eps of that sum on the three conduction
states, while the grouped sum alone under-states it by 1e3 to 1e5, the
conduction divergence being a small difference of three large face terms.
Today the absolute term never decides: the achieved \textbar R\textbar{} is 1e4 below
rtol S\_E. The {[}0.5, 2{]} T\_in band is removed from the column (it guards the
cell-by-cell Brent solve, which keeps it, and had refused a root, section
55). Outcome \texttt{converged\_on\_residual}; reason \texttt{residual\_above\_tolerance}
with the worst cell\textquotesingle s term table; record fields \texttt{column\_worst\_cell},
\texttt{column\_worst\_residual}, \texttt{column\_worst\_residual\_rel}.

\textbf{Cells without a marching root.} Cells whose marching Brent step finds no
root (its bracket {[}0.05, 4{]} T\_in) are column unknowns by default and the
residual decides (\texttt{cells\_marching\_unrooted} in the record;
\texttt{EXHALE\_ADV\_COLUMN\_EXCLUDE\_UNROOTED=1} restores the old set, measurement
only). On the photochemical s1 state three cells at 24.5-28.5 R\_p have
their roots at 4.0-4.3 T\_in; with them in the column every pass solves the
same set (the 354/351 alternation is gone).

\textbf{Refused rows carry the solution.} The status-3 rows of that product
(1.0-1.09 R\_p) were T-unsupported but composition-corrected, and their
corrected n(He 2\^{}3S) was 70 to 5600 times the run\textquotesingle s: the molecule-free
advection systems omit the He(2\^{}3S) + H2 destruction that the solution\textquotesingle s
balance includes. A composition class (formed with the particle-count class
on the state handed in, no threshold) now refuses a cell whose H+, He+ or
He(2\^{}3S) loses faster to H2 (R10 + R13, R17 + R23, \texttt{ioniz\_HeI23S\_H2}, from
\texttt{mol\_rates}) than to the carried channels; the class is applied inside the
passes, so the cascade takes the run\textquotesingle s composition as its history there;
the metal re-solve of \texttt{pp\_metals\ =\ 2} skips refused cells; and every refused
row is written with the run\textquotesingle s exact numbers (species and metal stages when
the composition field is refused; T when the T field is 1, 3 or 4; p, heat,
cool when both are refused): max relative difference 0 against the
solution\textquotesingle s columns on the three states. The transit census still counts
them as refused (173 rows to 1.089 R\_p, photochemical; 246 rows to 1.322
R\_p, scalar He/H 1.45).

\textbf{Results} (evaluate-only, \texttt{ew\_of\_state.sh}, tree ifx binary; EW in
percent-angstrom, adv / solution):

\textbar{} state \textbar{} column \textbar{} unknowns (unrooted) \textbar{} worst \textbar R\textbar/S\_E \textbar{} EW adv \textbar{} EW solution \textbar{}
\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar-\/-\/-\textbar{}
\textbar{} fiducial atomic full-equation \textbar{} converged \textbar{} 500 (0) \textbar{} 2.8e-11 \textbar{} 0.6491 \textbar{} 7.5179 \textbar{}
\textbar{} molecular photochemical He/H 9, conduction \textbar{} converged \textbar{} 329 (3) \textbar{} 1.6e-11 \textbar{} \textbf{0.7705} \textbar{} 8.5630 \textbar{}
\textbar{} molecular scalar He/H 1.45, conduction \textbar{} converged \textbar{} 256 (0) \textbar{} 2.9e-12 \textbar{} \textbf{0.1408} \textbar{} 2.1471 \textbar{}

All three exit 0 with the derived product accepted (\texttt{outer\ =\ unverified}
still: the outer iteration is the fixed ten passes, plan B3). The
photochemical adv EW of an ACCEPTED corrected profile is 0.7705 against
1.1032 of the refused marching profile (section 51, now superseded) and
the observed 1.108 +- 0.029: 30 per cent low. Its He 10830 line-center
optical depth peaks at 0.59 at b = 1.2 R\_p (the solution\textquotesingle s at 1.45), 85 per
cent of it from 1.1-3 R\_p, where T\_adv/T\_run is 1.00-1.04 and n(He 2\^{}3S)
is 0.93 to 0.60 of the solution\textquotesingle s (the advected, non-equilibrium
population); T\_adv reaches 2 to 4.4 T\_run beyond 10 R\_p, which carries
under 1 per cent of tau. The correction of the scalar He/H 1.45 product
moved from 0.1525 to 0.1408 (-7.7 per cent) because its composition class
reaches 1.32 R\_p. Tests: \texttt{adv\_static\_limit} 62 PASS (63 before: rows K
have five reasons now), \texttt{evaluate\_entry.sh} 25/25.
